The target's lack of capacity to face the force closes the mechanism: there is a grip and no adequate counter-grip.","ayah_refs":["69:10","69:3","69:4","69:7","69:6","69:9"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Impact becomes capture and defeat","parent_semantic_invariant":"A blow is followed through its full sequence: collision, opposed force, gripping control, collapse, and exhausted aftermath.","parent_surprising_reach":"The sequence reaches from knocking at a door and the clang of iron to wrestling holds, battlefield lines, uprooted bodies, and a road that consumes its traveler.","key":"C","name":"Rout, bodily fall, and spent capacity","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"The defeated body is thrown down, loses motion and power, and remains as the trace of overwhelming pursuit.","active_motifs":"الطرح والسقوط إلى الأرض (`quranic:root_000859:B001/m01`); العلة التي تصرع صاحبها (`quranic:root_000859:B002/m01`); السقوط إلى الهلاك والفناء (`quranic:root_001596:B001/m01`); إلقاء النفس في التهلكة (`quranic:root_001596:B002/m01`); استهلاك الجهد واستفراغه (`quranic:root_001596:B009/m01`); التهلك في المفازة تحيرا (`quranic:root_001596:B010/m01`); وادي تهلّك في الباطل (`quranic:root_001596:B011/m01`); ضعف وقصور عن الفعل (`quranic:root_000985:B001/m01`); فوت وسبق وإفلات من الطلب (`quranic:root_000985:B002/m01`); جمود ووقوف وكلال (`quranic:root_001273:B016/m01`); عين قائمة ذاهبة البصر (`quranic:root_001273:B021/m01`); الموت بلفظ الإراحة (`quranic:root_000609:B016/m01`); أثر الدبر والركوب (`quranic:root_001675:B010/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ص ر ع`: 69:7 (صَرْعَىٰ); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ); `ع ج ز`: 69:7 (أَعْجَازُ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)","synthesis":"Being felled joins ordinary defeat, seizure-like collapse, annihilation, self-endangerment, exhausted effort, bewildered circling, and falling into nullity. Inability and failed escape leave movement stalled; sight can remain physically intact yet functionally gone, death becomes an 'easing,' and the surviving body bears the abrasion of the ride.","ayah_refs":["69:7","69:5","69:6","69:15"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Air carries life, cold, and summons","parent_semantic_invariant":"Moving air can animate, overpower, cool, voice, inflate, or leave a body at the moment of death.","parent_surprising_reach":"One medium joins meteorology and physiology: gale, scent, lung, panting, hiccup, roar, and the final departing breath.","key":"A","name":"Cold gale, contrary currents, and lost force","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Air moves as a severe current whose direction, temperature, and dominance determine the scene.","active_motifs":"الريح والنسيم وهبوب الهواء (`quranic:root_000609:B003/m01`); الرائحة والشم والطيب والنتن (`quranic:root_000609:B004/m01`); الريح قوة وغلبة (`quranic:root_000609:B011/m01`); صرصرة الصوت (`quranic:root_000857:B002/m01`); ريح صرصر باردة (`quranic:root_000857:B004/m01`); رياح مؤتفكات مختلفة المهاب (`quranic:root_000041:B004/m01`); ريح تقابل الدبور (`quranic:root_001198:B012/m01`); طغيان الماء وما يجري مجراه (`quranic:root_000936:B002/m01`); علو الماء والقوة الجارفة (`quranic:root_000937:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ); `ء ف ك`: 69:9 (مُؤْتَفِكَٰتُ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ط غ ي`: 69:5 (طَّاغِيَةِ), 69:11 (طَغَا)","synthesis":"The wind is not an abstract weather label: it blows, smells, roars, chills, changes direction, meets a contrary current, and can become the very figure of superior force. Flood and gale share the operation of rising until ordinary orientation and resistance fail.","ayah_refs":["69:6","69:9","69:5","69:11"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Air carries life, cold, and summons","parent_semantic_invariant":"Moving air can animate, overpower, cool, voice, inflate, or leave a body at the moment of death.","parent_surprising_reach":"One medium joins meteorology and physiology: gale, scent, lung, panting, hiccup, roar, and the final departing breath.","key":"B","name":"Breath, lung, inflation, and final exhalation","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Air enters and leaves living bodies, expands tissue, strains respiration, and marks the loss of life.","active_motifs":"النفس والروح التي بها حياة البدن (`quranic:root_000609:B001/m01`); الراحة والاستراحة والإراحة (`quranic:root_000609:B007/m01`); الموت بلفظ الإراحة (`quranic:root_000609:B016/m01`); الرئة وما يصيبها (`quranic:root_000531:B009/m01`); إرسال الريح في الشيء (`quranic:root_001529:B001/m01`); انتفاخ الشيء وامتلاؤه بالريح (`quranic:root_001529:B002/m01`); انتفاخ الفخر والكبر (`quranic:root_001529:B006/m01`); تصعد النفس وانتفاخه (`quranic:root_000537:B004/m01`); فواق الصدر والنزع (`quranic:root_001188:B010/m01`); عرض الإنسان حماه وبدنه وظاهر وجهه (`quranic:root_001001:B009/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ع ر ض`: 69:18 (تُعْرَضُ)","synthesis":"Spirit, lung, inhaled or expelled air, swelling, proud inflation, panting, hiccup, and the death-rattle occupy one respiratory mechanism. Rest is the recovery of breath, while death is its terminal release; even social pride is materialized as a body puffed beyond its proper measure.","ayah_refs":["69:6","69:7","69:8","69:13","69:10","69:17","69:18"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Air carries life, cold, and summons","parent_semantic_invariant":"Moving air can animate, overpower, cool, voice, inflate, or leave a body at the moment of death.","parent_surprising_reach":"One medium joins meteorology and physiology: gale, scent, lung, panting, hiccup, roar, and the final departing breath.","key":"C","name":"Blast, roar, and receptive ear","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A forceful emission becomes signal, and a listening organ must receive and register it.","active_motifs":"إرسال الريح في الشيء (`quranic:root_001529:B001/m01`); قرن النفخ (`quranic:root_000891:B004/m01`); صرصرة الصوت (`quranic:root_000857:B002/m01`); الخَوَاة صوتا (`quranic:root_000450:B007/m01`); ارتفاع الصوت والجلبة (`quranic:root_001664:B005/m01`); الأذن الجارحة وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_000022:B001/m01`); الإصغاء وقبول المسموع (`quranic:root_000022:B002/m01`); العلم والإعلام بالنداء (`quranic:root_000022:B003/m01`); طغي القوم صوتهم (`quranic:root_000936:B008/m01`); شقشقة البعير والخطيب (`quranic:root_000807:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ); `ص و ر`: 69:13 (صُّورِ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ); `خ و ي`: 69:7 (خَاوِيَةٍ); `و ع ي`: 69:12 (تَعِيَ/وَٰعِيَةٌ); `ء ذ ن`: 69:12 (أُذُنٌ); `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ش ق ق`: 69:16 (ٱنشَقَّتِ)","synthesis":"Blowing into the horn turns breath into a public acoustic event. Roar, clamor, collective voice, and the camel's or orator's protruding throat-sound converge on an ear that is both a physical receiver and an act of obedient attention; the call becomes knowledge when announced and heard. The `ط غ و` voice motif has no available surface-root anchor.","ayah_refs":["69:13","69:6","69:7","69:12","69:16"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Air carries life, cold, and summons","parent_semantic_invariant":"Moving air can animate, overpower, cool, voice, inflate, or leave a body at the moment of death.","parent_surprising_reach":"One medium joins meteorology and physiology: gale, scent, lung, panting, hiccup, roar, and the final departing breath.","key":"D","name":"Relief, alternation, and the return of motion","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"After strain, breath and activity return by rest, alternating labor, or an evening homecoming.","active_motifs":"الرواح والعشي ورد الماشية (`quranic:root_000609:B005/m01`); الراحة والاستراحة والإراحة (`quranic:root_000609:B007/m01`); المراوحة والتناوب (`quranic:root_000609:B008/m01`); السعة والانبساط الحسي (`quranic:root_000609:B009/m01`); الأريحية والنشاط للمعروف (`quranic:root_000609:B010/m01`); أوبة الوعي والقوة والراحة (`quranic:root_001188:B005/m01`); رجوع اللبن بين الحلبتين (`quranic:root_001188:B006/m01`); أخذ الشيء جرعات أو دفعات (`quranic:root_001188:B007/m01`); رجوع بعد انصراف وتثنية بعد بدء (`quranic:root_001058:B001/m01`); عادة ودرَبة ومواظبة (`quranic:root_001058:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ)","synthesis":"Evening return, rest, alternating work, bodily expansion, and renewed generosity describe recovery as regained room to act. Awakening after illness and the returning milk between milkings give the same rhythm a physiological and pastoral form, while recurrence becomes habit through repeated performance.","ayah_refs":["69:6","69:17","69:4"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Vertical forms are hollowed, split, and leveled","parent_semantic_invariant":"Tall bodies, palms, buildings, mountains, and sky lose their interior support and vertical distinction.","parent_surprising_reach":"Ruin is rendered through carpentry and anatomy: empty belly, uprooted trunk, loosened wall, torn skin, buried well, and sand ridge all undergo related structural failure.","key":"D","name":"Barren vacancy after failed rain","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Rain misses or withdraws, growth fails, and place becomes empty, exposed, and lethal to a traveler.","active_motifs":"أرض مأفوكة صرف عنها المطر (`quranic:root_000041:B005/m01`); أرض أخطأها المطر (`quranic:root_000420:B003/m01`); أرض هلكين لا غيث فيها (`quranic:root_001596:B005/m01`); مهواة ومفازة مهلكة (`quranic:root_001596:B006/m01`); إخلاف النوء (`quranic:root_000450:B003/m01`); خواء الجوف (`quranic:root_000450:B005/m01`); قرع وانكشاف وخلو (`quranic:root_001219:B008/m01`); دوام الشيء وبقاؤه (`quranic:root_000142:B001/m01`); البقية وما يبقى من الشيء (`quranic:root_000142:B002/m01`); الإبقاء عفوا واستحياء (`quranic:root_000142:B003/m01`); حبس بعض الشيء وادخاره (`quranic:root_000142:B004/m01`); يبس الشجر (`quranic:root_000217:B008/m01`); ما بالدار نافخ ضرمة (`quranic:root_001529:B005/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ء ف ك`: 69:9 (مُؤْتَفِكَٰتُ); `خ ط ء`: 69:9 (خَاطِئَةِ); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ); `خ و ي`: 69:7 (خَاوِيَةٍ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ب ق ي`: 69:8 (بَاقِيَةٍ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ)","synthesis":"A rainless, burned tract and a star-season that yields no water lead to hollow bellies, dead wood, stripped enclosures, an empty house, and a killing waste. Against that extinction, remaining, remnant, mercy, and saved reserve become sharply material counter-images: survival is what has not been stripped out of the place.","ayah_refs":["69:9","69:5","69:6","69:7","69:4","69:8","69:14","69:13"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Flood becomes conveyance and controlled water","parent_semantic_invariant":"Water moves from excess to transport, capture, storage, and measured delivery.","parent_surprising_reach":"The flood scene opens into hydraulic engineering: channels, catch basins, wells, receiving hands, cloud-reservoirs, and reflective coatings.","key":"C","name":"Cloud reservoir and released rain","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Water accumulates overhead, then breaks from the cloud in repeated or forceful release.","active_motifs":"رباب السحاب (`quranic:root_000532:B008/m01`); الحَمَل في السماء والسحاب (`quranic:root_000357:B010/m01`); أفاويق السحاب دفعات الماء (`quranic:root_001188:B008/m01`); انبعاق عزالي السحاب بالمطر (`quranic:root_001688:B003/m01`); وقوع المطر ومساقطه (`quranic:root_001675:B003/m01`); الريحان النبات والورق (`quranic:root_000609:B012/m01`); تفطر الشجر وطول النبات (`quranic:root_000609:B017/m01`); علو الشيء وربو الأرض (`quranic:root_001529:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `و ه ي`: 69:16 (وَاهِيَةٌ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ)","synthesis":"Layered cloud and a dark water-bearing mass store the supply; cloud-drafts and burst rain release it. Falling water produces spring swelling, leafing, and plant height, so the same overhead reservoir that can overwhelm also renews the ground.","ayah_refs":["69:10","69:17","69:11","69:14","69:16","69:15","69:6","69:13"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Falsehood redirects signs and surfaces","parent_semantic_invariant":"Misrepresentation works by attributing what is not so, coating reality with a misleading surface, or turning perception away from its proper object.","parent_surprising_reach":"Falsehood reaches bodily motion and materials: milk promises continuity then stops, an animal runs then checks, cloth lies by its colors, metal is plated, and a charm turns a face.","key":"D","name":"Promised continuation that abruptly fails","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"An action appears likely to persist but stops, delays, or proves unable to complete its course.","active_motifs":"ما كذب أن فعل أي ما لبث (`quranic:root_001290:B005/m01`); كذب لبن الناقة إذا ذهب ولم يدم (`quranic:root_001290:B006/m01`); كذب الوحشي إذا جرى ثم وقف (`quranic:root_001290:B007/m01`); سكوت لا يبدئ ولا يعيد (`quranic:root_001058:B003/m01`); ضعف وقصور عن الفعل (`quranic:root_000985:B001/m01`); فوت وسبق وإفلات من الطلب (`quranic:root_000985:B002/m01`); جمود ووقوف وكلال (`quranic:root_001273:B016/m01`); ترقب الشيء وانتظاره بالبصر (`quranic:root_000142:B005/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ك ذ ب`: 69:4 (كَذَّبَتْ); `ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ع ج ز`: 69:7 (أَعْجَازُ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ب ق ي`: 69:8 (بَاقِيَةٍ)","synthesis":"Immediate action can belie delay, milk can belie expected duration, and a running animal can halt to look back. Speechlessness, incapacity, escape, and a stalled mount turn failed continuation into a bodily scene; watchful waiting measures the gap between expectation and arrival.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:6","69:7","69:8"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Herd life follows generation, movement, and equipment","parent_semantic_invariant":"Animal life is organized through mating, gestation, maturation, herd circulation, milking intervals, shelter, and predation.","parent_surprising_reach":"The lexical network turns abstract completion and return into a full pastoral economy, from conception and birth through age, watering, tack, shelter, and the hunter's blind.","key":"A","name":"Mating, conception, and approaching birth","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Male and female animals meet, conception becomes visible, and a carried young approaches emergence.","active_motifs":"ناقة بلغت حق الحمل والانتفاع (`quranic:root_000347:B008/m01`); تمام حال الحيوان وقوته (`quranic:root_000347:B013/m01`); أحق من الخيل يطابق خطوه أو يشتد بدنه (`quranic:root_000347:B014/m01`); ضراب الفحل وإنزاؤه (`quranic:root_001219:B003/m01`); ظهور حمل الناقة أو الشاة (`quranic:root_000531:B010/m01`); تحصن الفرس وصيرورته فحلا (`quranic:root_000609:B018/m01`); الحَمْل الباطن والثمر (`quranic:root_000357:B002/m01`); الحَمَل من الضأن (`quranic:root_000357:B009/m01`); ماء الفحل في الرحم (`quranic:root_001458:B004/m01`); اشتهاء الأنثى للفحل (`quranic:root_000248:B009/m01`); فرخ النعام (`quranic:root_000248:B010/m01`); الحامل تدنو ولادتها (`quranic:root_000548:B005/m01`); رفع الحمار رأسه عند الحمل (`quranic:root_001000:B009/m01`); تغذية ونشوء (`quranic:root_000537:B005/m01`); انتفاخ الشيء وامتلاؤه بالريح (`quranic:root_001529:B002/m01`); الطغيا الصغير من بقر الوحش (`quranic:root_000936:B009/m01`); تطاول الفحل على الشول (`quranic:root_000745:B003/m01`); إلقاء الثقل في المخالطة (`quranic:root_000482:B009/m01`); المواقعة في الجماع (`quranic:root_001675:B014/m01`); ترية الحيض (`quranic:root_000531:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ر ء ي`: 69:7 (تَرَى), 69:8 (تَرَىٰ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ); `ر ج و`: 69:17 (أَرْجَآئِ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ); `ط غ و`: unavailable (surface root missing); `س م و`: 69:16 (سَّمَآءُ); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ)","synthesis":"Breeding readiness, mounting, deposited seed, visible pregnancy, inward burden, swelling, menstrual sign, and the nearness of birth form a continuous generational scene. A stallion rising over mares and bodily weight in intercourse make coupling explicit; young camel, lamb, ostrich chick, and wild-cattle calf mark successive products. The `ط غ و` calf motif has no available surface-root anchor.","ayah_refs":["69:1","69:2","69:3","69:4","69:7","69:8","69:6","69:11","69:14","69:17","69:12","69:10","69:13","69:16","69:15"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Herd life follows generation, movement, and equipment","parent_semantic_invariant":"Animal life is organized through mating, gestation, maturation, herd circulation, milking intervals, shelter, and predation.","parent_surprising_reach":"The lexical network turns abstract completion and return into a full pastoral economy, from conception and birth through age, watering, tack, shelter, and the hunter's blind.","key":"B","name":"Age, bodily form, and usable maturity","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"An animal passes from youth into a bodily state suited to work, breeding, or valuation.","active_motifs":"عود مسن فيه بقايا قوة (`quranic:root_001058:B008/m01`); كبر وقدم حتى يسمى عجوزا (`quranic:root_000985:B003/m01`); آخر الولد عند كبر الأبوين (`quranic:root_000985:B007/m01`); العُتِيّ من الكبر (`quranic:root_000981:B002/m01`); غلظ الخلقة والجسم (`quranic:root_000217:B003/m01`); خلقة مطبوعة (`quranic:root_000217:B004/m01`); قامة وقوام الجسم والطول (`quranic:root_001273:B011/m01`); السعة والانبساط الحسي (`quranic:root_000609:B009/m01`); ناقة بلغت حق الحمل والانتفاع (`quranic:root_000347:B008/m01`); تمام حال الحيوان وقوته (`quranic:root_000347:B013/m01`); أحق من الخيل يطابق خطوه أو يشتد بدنه (`quranic:root_000347:B014/m01`); صرصور البختي (`quranic:root_000857:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ع ج ز`: 69:7 (أَعْجَازُ); `ع ت و`: 69:6 (عَاتِيَةٍ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ)","synthesis":"Old camel, late child, extreme age, thick bodily formation, inherited disposition, stature, breadth of foot, Bactrian camel, completed fatness, and matched stride describe maturity as a change in usable form. The same completion may qualify an animal for load, breeding, or price.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:6","69:7","69:14","69:1","69:2","69:3"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Herd life follows generation, movement, and equipment","parent_semantic_invariant":"Animal life is organized through mating, gestation, maturation, herd circulation, milking intervals, shelter, and predation.","parent_surprising_reach":"The lexical network turns abstract completion and return into a full pastoral economy, from conception and birth through age, watering, tack, shelter, and the hunter's blind.","key":"C","name":"Herd return, alternating groups, and milking rhythm","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Animals move out and back in ordered groups, pause at water, and yield milk on recurring intervals.","active_motifs":"رجوع بعد انصراف وتثنية بعد بدء (`quranic:root_001058:B001/m01`); عيادة ومعادة وزيارة راجعة (`quranic:root_001058:B005/m01`); الرواح والعشي ورد الماشية (`quranic:root_000609:B005/m01`); المراوحة والتناوب (`quranic:root_000609:B008/m01`); طرفا النهار المتعاقبان (`quranic:root_000859:B005/m01`); إبل تختلف ذهابا ومجيئا (`quranic:root_000859:B006/m01`); انتصاب وقيام بالبدن (`quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`); جمود ووقوف وكلال (`quranic:root_001273:B016/m01`); انتصاف النهار وقائم الظهيرة (`quranic:root_001273:B017/m01`); قوام في قوائم الشاة (`quranic:root_001273:B020/m01`); رجوع اللبن بين الحلبتين (`quranic:root_001188:B006/m01`); أخذ الشيء جرعات أو دفعات (`quranic:root_001188:B007/m01`); السير السهل واللين (`quranic:root_000563:B003/m01`); التتابع والقطع (`quranic:root_000563:B005/m01`); اللبن والدر المتتابع (`quranic:root_000563:B006/m01`); أداة الحمل ومركوبه (`quranic:root_000357:B006/m01`); العدد سبعة وما يتفرع منه (`quranic:root_000669:B001/m01`); تكسّر التهالك في المشي والغنج (`quranic:root_001596:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ص ر ع`: 69:7 (صَرْعَىٰ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ); `ر س ل`: 69:10 (رَسُولَ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `س ب ع`: 69:7 (سَبْعَ); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ)","synthesis":"Return and repeated visitation become pastoral circulation: morning and evening groups cross, one herd arrives as another leaves, animals stand or tire, and the day reaches its central station. Milk returns between milkings and is taken in intervals; easy or swaying gait, successive droves, abundant yield, transport stock, and numbered watering cycles maintain the rhythm.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:6","69:7","69:17","69:10","69:11","69:14","69:5"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Plants are raised, selected, and made valuable","parent_semantic_invariant":"Cultivation converts water and soil into growth, then separates, selects, stores, and values the product.","parent_surprising_reach":"Palm trunks open onto seedbed, cloud, sap, sieve, jewelry, market, and reserved running strength rather than remaining a single botanical emblem.","key":"A","name":"Watered swelling and vegetative renewal","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Moisture enters soil and plant, producing swelling, leafing, fruit, and sustained green matter.","active_motifs":"الأرض اللينة المنبتة (`quranic:root_000025:B002/m01`); زيادة وعلو (`quranic:root_000537:B001/m01`); تغذية ونشوء (`quranic:root_000537:B005/m01`); الريحان النبات والورق (`quranic:root_000609:B012/m01`); تفطر الشجر وطول النبات (`quranic:root_000609:B017/m01`); ظهور الماء ودخوله وكثرته (`quranic:root_001458:B002/m01`); إيصال الماء بالسقي والصب (`quranic:root_001458:B003/m01`); رونق كالماء في الوجه والكلام والثمر (`quranic:root_001458:B006/m01`); رباب السحاب (`quranic:root_000532:B008/m01`); ربة نبات (`quranic:root_000532:B012/m01`); الحَمْل الباطن والثمر (`quranic:root_000357:B002/m01`); الحَمَل في السماء والسحاب (`quranic:root_000357:B010/m01`); علو الشيء وربو الأرض (`quranic:root_001529:B003/m01`); يبس الشجر (`quranic:root_000217:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ); `ر ب و`: 69:10 (رَّابِيَةً); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `م و ه`: 69:11 (مَآءُ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ح م ل`: 69:11 (حَمَلْ), 69:14 (حُمِلَتِ), 69:17 (يَحْمِلُ); `ن ف خ`: 69:13 (نُفِخَ/نَفْخَةٌ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ)","synthesis":"Soft fertile earth, rising growth, nourished young, leaves, lengthening shoots, water-filled fruit, rain cloud, persistent green plant, fruit burden, and spring swelling form one growth process. Dry wood marks its arrested state, while cloud and carried water explain the material source of renewal.","ayah_refs":["69:14","69:10","69:6","69:11","69:17","69:13"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Plants are raised, selected, and made valuable","parent_semantic_invariant":"Cultivation converts water and soil into growth, then separates, selects, stores, and values the product.","parent_surprising_reach":"Palm trunks open onto seedbed, cloud, sap, sieve, jewelry, market, and reserved running strength rather than remaining a single botanical emblem.","key":"D","name":"Garden produce, insects, and damaged wood","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A cultivated plot contains edible growth alongside small creatures that inhabit or damage it.","active_motifs":"قرع اليقطين (`quranic:root_001219:B009/m01`); دويبة الجعلان (`quranic:root_000248:B008/m01`); دويبة الصرصر (`quranic:root_000857:B001/m01`); الأَرَضَة آكلة الخشب (`quranic:root_000025:B010/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ج ع ل`: 69:12 (نَجْعَلَ); `ص ر ص ر`: 69:6 (صَرْصَرٍ); `ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ)","synthesis":"Gourd, dung beetle, small chirping creature, and wood-eating termite create a compact garden ecology. The plant is not isolated produce: it shares soil and timber with organisms that consume, inhabit, or acoustically mark the plot.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:12","69:6","69:14"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Time is counted through recurrence and completion","parent_semantic_invariant":"Duration becomes intelligible through numbered units, returning cycles, and thresholds at which an action or state is complete.","parent_surprising_reach":"Calendar units connect to lunar stations, milking intervals, noon balance, complete fatness, festival return, and the point at which delay expires.","key":"B","name":"Lunar, stellar, diurnal, and pastoral cycles","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Recurring natural positions regulate expectation, movement, and replenishment.","active_motifs":"أخذ القمر في منازله (`quranic:root_000018:B008/m01`); كواكب العرش (`quranic:root_001000:B008/m01`); انتصاف النهار وقائم الظهيرة (`quranic:root_001273:B017/m01`); الرواح والعشي ورد الماشية (`quranic:root_000609:B005/m01`); طرفا النهار المتعاقبان (`quranic:root_000859:B005/m01`); رجوع اللبن بين الحلبتين (`quranic:root_001188:B006/m01`); أخذ الشيء جرعات أو دفعات (`quranic:root_001188:B007/m01`); أفاويق السحاب دفعات الماء (`quranic:root_001188:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ء خ ذ`: 69:10 (أَخَذَ/أَخْذَةً); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ); `ص ر ع`: 69:7 (صَرْعَىٰ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ)","synthesis":"The moon takes successive stations, constellations mark the sky, noon reaches balance, and evening initiates return. Morning and evening pair, milk and cloud-water replenish in intervals, and repeated small portions turn recurrence into a usable temporal measure.","ayah_refs":["69:10","69:17","69:7","69:6"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Time is counted through recurrence and completion","parent_semantic_invariant":"Duration becomes intelligible through numbered units, returning cycles, and thresholds at which an action or state is complete.","parent_surprising_reach":"Calendar units connect to lunar stations, milking intervals, noon balance, complete fatness, festival return, and the point at which delay expires.","key":"C","name":"Threshold, termination, and completed state","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"A process reaches the point where it becomes binding, finished, irreversible, or due.","active_motifs":"قطع حاسم (`quranic:root_000322:B001/m01`); تمام الزمن (`quranic:root_000482:B007/m01`); لزوم واجب واستحقاق ثابت (`quranic:root_000347:B002/m01`); تمام حال الحيوان وقوته (`quranic:root_000347:B013/m01`); تقدم الشيء أو إقباله (`quranic:root_001198:B002/m01`); ابتداء حاضر غير مهيأ (`quranic:root_001198:B015/m01`); إرجاء الشيء إلى وقت لاحق (`quranic:root_000548:B004/m01`); سقوط الشيء وثبوته (`quranic:root_001675:B001/m01`); العُتِيّ من الكبر (`quranic:root_000981:B002/m01`); العرض الطارئ الذي يعرض ثم يزول (`quranic:root_001001:B007/m01`); علو المقدار والحد (`quranic:root_001188:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ح س م`: 69:7 (حُسُومًا); `د ك ك`: 69:14 (دُكَّ/دَكَّةً); `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ق ب ل`: 69:9 (قَبْلَ); `ر ج و`: 69:17 (أَرْجَآئِ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ع ت و`: 69:6 (عَاتِيَةٍ); `ع ر ض`: 69:18 (تُعْرَضُ); `ف و ق`: 69:17 (فَوْقَ)","synthesis":"Decisive cutting, a complete span, binding entitlement, finished bodily condition, precedence, fresh beginning, postponement, fixed occurrence, passing condition, quantified upper limit, and terminal age all locate thresholds. The same logic explains why the event can be both awaited and, once arrived, no longer negotiable.","ayah_refs":["69:7","69:14","69:1","69:2","69:3","69:9","69:17","69:15","69:6","69:18"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Time is counted through recurrence and completion","parent_semantic_invariant":"Duration becomes intelligible through numbered units, returning cycles, and thresholds at which an action or state is complete.","parent_surprising_reach":"Calendar units connect to lunar stations, milking intervals, noon balance, complete fatness, festival return, and the point at which delay expires.","key":"D","name":"Return, habit, visitation, and festival","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Repeated arrival turns isolated time into custom, social observance, and remembered return.","active_motifs":"رجوع بعد انصراف وتثنية بعد بدء (`quranic:root_001058:B001/m01`); مصير ومرجع ومعاد (`quranic:root_001058:B002/m01`); عادة ودرَبة ومواظبة (`quranic:root_001058:B004/m01`); عيادة ومعادة وزيارة راجعة (`quranic:root_001058:B005/m01`); عيد وحال يعاود (`quranic:root_001058:B007/m01`); مقام وإقامة في موضع (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`); الرواح والعشي ورد الماشية (`quranic:root_000609:B005/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ)","synthesis":"Return after departure becomes destination, habit, sick-visit, recurring assembly, and festival. A place of staying and evening homecoming anchor recurrence spatially, so time is remembered by where bodies gather again.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:6","69:7"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Time is counted through recurrence and completion","parent_semantic_invariant":"Duration becomes intelligible through numbered units, returning cycles, and thresholds at which an action or state is complete.","parent_surprising_reach":"Calendar units connect to lunar stations, milking intervals, noon balance, complete fatness, festival return, and the point at which delay expires.","key":"E","name":"Expected resurrection and singular sovereignty","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Hope and expectation look toward the future rising in which singular rule is fully manifest.","active_motifs":"تطلع القلب إلى المأمول (`quranic:root_000548:B001/m01`); توقع الأمر ورمي الظن (`quranic:root_001675:B012/m01`); قيامة وبعث وقيام الساعة (`quranic:root_001273:B013/m01`); التفرد بالنظير والفضل (`quranic:root_001631:B003/m01`); وحدانية الله بلا شريك (`quranic:root_001631:B004/m01`); إما هلكت هلك على كل حال (`quranic:root_001596:B008/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ر ج و`: 69:17 (أَرْجَآئِ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `و ح د`: 69:13 (وَٰحِدَةٌ), 69:14 (وَٰحِدَةً); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ)","synthesis":"Hope, expectation, resurrection, incomparability, divine unity, and the formula 'whatever the manner of death' gather around the coming event. Future time is not empty projection: it is oriented toward a rising governed by the one without partner.","ayah_refs":["69:17","69:15","69:7","69:13","69:14","69:5","69:6"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Passage is oriented by fronts, roads, and edges","parent_semantic_invariant":"Movement depends on facing a destination, entering a route, negotiating boundaries, and finding a station or return point.","parent_surprising_reach":"The route includes mountain tracks, fatal wastes, courtyard approaches, receiving points, sky margins, body sides, and a horse that cannot run straight.","key":"B","name":"Old road, mountain track, and killing waste","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A traveler follows a known line through inhabited and dangerous terrain.","active_motifs":"قدم وطريق عود (`quranic:root_001058:B009/m01`); قارعة الطريق والدار (`quranic:root_001219:B010/m01`); مقام وإقامة في موضع (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`); استقامة واعتدال واستواء (`quranic:root_001273:B008/m01`); العادة والطريقة الجارية (`quranic:root_000240:B002/m01`); السير السهل واللين (`quranic:root_000563:B003/m01`); مهواة ومفازة مهلكة (`quranic:root_001596:B006/m01`); التهلك في المفازة تحيرا (`quranic:root_001596:B010/m01`); عروض ونواح وأسماء فنية أو موضعية (`quranic:root_001001:B014/m01`); دخول الجبال (`quranic:root_000217:B006/m01`); مصادفة رمل عريض (`quranic:root_000217:B011/m01`); حقحقة تجهد الظهر في السير (`quranic:root_000347:B012/m01`); ثقل يشق النفس (`quranic:root_000807:B003/m01`); الشقة البعيدة (`quranic:root_000807:B005/m01`); مَلَك الطريق والوادي (`quranic:root_001444:B006/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ع و د`: 69:4 (عَادٌۢ), 69:6 (عَادٌ); `ق ر ع`: 69:4 (قَارِعَةِ); `ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ج ر ي`: 69:11 (جَارِيَةِ); `ر س ل`: 69:10 (رَسُولَ); `ه ل ك`: 69:5 (أُهْلِكُ), 69:6 (أُهْلِكُ); `ع ر ض`: 69:18 (تُعْرَضُ); `ج ب ل`: 69:14 (جِبَالُ); `ح ق ق`: 69:1 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:2 (حَآقَّةُ), 69:3 (حَآقَّةُ); `ش ق ق`: 69:16 (ٱنشَقَّتِ); `م ل ك`: 69:17 (مَلَكُ)","synthesis":"Ancient road, exposed courtyard or thoroughfare, established way, personal route, easy gait, mountain entry, broad sand barrier, distant journey, road-center, and lethal wilderness make a continuous travel scene. Back-breaking pace and hardship test the traveler; straightness is the norm, while bewildered circling is its failure.","ayah_refs":["69:4","69:6","69:7","69:11","69:10","69:5","69:18","69:14","69:1","69:2","69:3","69:16","69:17"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Passage is oriented by fronts, roads, and edges","parent_semantic_invariant":"Movement depends on facing a destination, entering a route, negotiating boundaries, and finding a station or return point.","parent_surprising_reach":"The route includes mountain tracks, fatal wastes, courtyard approaches, receiving points, sky margins, body sides, and a horse that cannot run straight.","key":"D","name":"Station, dwelling, and return to place","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Motion resolves into standing, lodging, persistent residence, or a place to which one returns.","active_motifs":"انتصاب وقيام بالبدن (`quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`); مقام وإقامة في موضع (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`); نيابة وقيام مقام غيره (`quranic:root_001273:B007/m01`); لزوم وإقامة ودوام (`quranic:root_000532:B007/m01`); الثبات في البلد (`quranic:root_001000:B012/m01`); لزوم الأرض والتثاقل إليها (`quranic:root_000025:B006/m01`); وقوع الطير وموضعه (`quranic:root_001675:B006/m01`); ربوض الدواب والإبل (`quranic:root_001675:B007/m01`); الرواح والعشي ورد الماشية (`quranic:root_000609:B005/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); `ر ب ب`: 69:10 (رَبِّ), 69:17 (رَبِّ); `ع ر ش`: 69:17 (عَرْشَ); `ء ر ض`: 69:14 (أَرْضُ); `و ق ع`: 69:15 (وَقَعَتِ/وَاقِعَةُ); `ر و ح`: 69:6 (رِيحٍ)","synthesis":"Standing, residence, substitution in another's place, settled duration, fixed dwelling, adherence to the ground, a bird's landing, an animal's kneeling, and evening homecoming turn location into the outcome of movement.","ayah_refs":["69:7","69:10","69:17","69:14","69:15","69:6"]},{"parent_channel":"P1: Community is sustained by kin care and exchange","parent_semantic_invariant":"Groups endure through lineage, guardianship, gift, valuation, labor, and the circulation of benefit.","parent_surprising_reach":"The social body includes outsider, foundling, stepchild, elder, clan, guarantor, wage, tribute, market, and returning favor.","key":"A","name":"Clan, tribe, crowd, and recognized elder","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"Individuals are placed inside lineage and assembled groups with visible leaders.","active_motifs":"جماعة الناس والرجال (`quranic:root_001273:B001/m01`); مقام وإقامة في موضع (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`); جماعة يقبل بعضها على بعض (`quranic:root_001198:B009/m01`); ربة وجماعات كثيرة (`quranic:root_000532:B004/m01`); رَبْرَب قطيع (`quranic:root_000532:B014/m01`); كثرة كالجبل (`quranic:root_000217:B002/m01`); سادات كالجِبال (`quranic:root_000217:B012/m01`); كثرة الرجال واجتماعهم (`quranic:root_001664:B008/m01`); الحميل المحمول في النسب (`quranic:root_000357:B005/m01`); آخر الولد عند كبر الأبوين (`quranic:root_000985:B007/m01`); عود مسن فيه بقايا قوة (`quranic:root_001058:B008/m01`); الخليق بالخير كالأرض الأريضة (`quranic:root_000025:B003/m01`); ابن الأرض الغريب (`quranic:root_000025:B004/m01`); أهل البيت من بني الأعمام (`quranic:root_000537:B007/m01`); عاد وأعلام منسوبة إليها (`quranic:root_001058:B012/m01`); نسبة الهالكي إلى الحداد (`quranic:root_001596:B007/m01`); انصداع الجماعة بالخلاف (`quranic:root_000807:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق و م`: 69:7 (قَوْمَ); ## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
